CR 24 (XP 62,000; PB +7)

Traits

Amphibious. The leviathan can breathe air and water.

Brine-Song Reflux. A creature that hits the leviathan with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 14 (4d6) Thunder damage and has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the leviathan fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The leviathan makes two Bulk Slam attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Deafening Undertow.

Bulk Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 10 ft. 36 (6d8 + 9) Bludgeoning damage.

Drowning Verse (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature in a 60-foot Cone. Failure: 78 (12d12) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Deafening Undertow. Strength Saving Throw: DC 24, one creature within 30 feet that is Deafened. Failure: 63 (14d8) Thunder damage. Success: Half damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the leviathan can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Undertow Surge. The leviathan moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Bulk Slam attack.

Keel Breaker. Strength Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature within 10 feet of the leviathan. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

[concept] identity: The leviathan opens a fleet-fight by sounding a subsonic verse that rolls through hull and marrow before a single limb strikes, staggering crews and drowning their coordination in dread. Every round after, it circles beneath the waterline and slams its brine-crusted bulk up through keels with tidal force, dragging shattered ships under rather than dueling anything that floats. It is not cruel but utterly territorial and patient, harrying a broken fleet for hours and only withdrawing when the water itself turns against it.
[concept] lore: Older than the drowned cities whose bells it answers, the leviathan sleeps in the lightless trench and rises only when the noise of a fleet above offends the silence it has kept for a thousand years.

Bog Hound
Medium beast, unaligned
Armor Class

11 (natural armor)

22 (3d8 + 9)

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

1 (200 XP)

+2

Faultless Tracker. The bog hound knows the direction and distance to any creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, as long as the two of them are on the same plane of existence.
Whelm. As a bonus action, the bog hound whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the bog hound, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the bog hound's turns. The bog hound can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the bog hound takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the bog hound must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the bog hound. If the bog hound dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The bog hound makes two Bite attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Mire Drag.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 5 (1d8 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 11). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Mire Drag: One creature within 20 feet of the bog hound must succeed on a DC 13 Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 15 feet straight toward the bog hound by the bog hound's jaws and be grappled (escape DC 11).
```

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages Common plus one other language

CR 3 (XP 700; PB +2)

Traits

Ethereal Sight. The chapel bell-ringer can see 60 feet into the Ethereal Plane when it is on the Material Plane.

Incorporeal Movement. The chapel bell-ringer can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.

Actions

Multiattack. The chapel bell-ringer makes two Withering Touch attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Horrific Visage.

Withering Touch. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 19 (3d10 + 3) Necrotic damage.

Horrific Visage. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 60-foot Cone that can see the chapel bell-ringer and isn't an Undead. Failure: 10 (2d6 + 3) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the start of the chapel bell-ringer's next turn. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Horrific Visage for 24 hours.

Possession (Recharge 6). Charisma Saving Throw: DC 13, one Humanoid the chapel bell-ringer can see within 5 feet. Failure: The target is possessed by the chapel bell-ringer; the chapel bell-ringer disappears, and the target has the Incapacitated condition and loses control of its body. The chapel bell-ringer now controls the body, but the target retains awareness. The chapel bell-ringer can't be targeted by any attack, spell, or other effect, except ones that specifically target Undead. The chapel bell-ringer's game statistics are the same, except it uses the possessed target's Speed, as well as the target's Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution modifiers.
The possession lasts until the body drops to 0 Hit Points or the chapel bell-ringer leaves as a Bonus Action. When the possession ends, the chapel bell-ringer appears in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target, and the target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours.

Etherealness. The chapel bell-ringer casts the Etherealness spell, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability. The chapel bell-ringer is visible on the Material Plane while on the Border Ethereal and vice versa, but it can't affect or be affected by anything on the other plane.

Swarm of Drowned Nave Choirs
Medium swarm of Tiny undead, lawful neutral
Armor Class

14 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

180 (24d8 + 72)

10 (5,900 XP)

+4

Choral Mockery. The swarm can mimic animal sounds and humanoid voices. A creature that hears the sounds can tell they are imitations only with a successful DC 15 Wisdom (Insight) check.
Swarm. The swarm can occupy another creature's space and vice versa, and the swarm can move through any opening large enough for a Tiny creature. The swarm can't regain hit points or gain temporary hit points.

Actions
Strike Hands: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 61 (13d8 + 3) necrotic damage, or 30 (6d8 + 3) necrotic damage if the swarm has half of its hit points or fewer.
Inviting Hymn (Recharge 5-6): Each creature of the swarm's choice within 60 feet that can see or hear the choir's hymn must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be charmed until the start of the swarm's next turn, and while charmed this way, it must move toward the swarm by the safest available route on its turn and can't take reactions.

Adult Red Dragon
Huge dragon, chaotic evil
Armor Class

19 (natural armor)

256 (19d12 + 133)

Common, Draconic

Challenge

17 (18,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (1d10 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

```
Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

195 (23d8 + 92)

Speed: 

40 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

15 (+2)

	

18 (+4)

Skills: 

Perception +7, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, WIS +7

Damage Resistances: 

necrotic

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Thornwrithed Reprisal. A creature that touches the vampire or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) necrotic damage.
Hedge Slip. When a creature the vampire can see ends its turn within 5 feet of it, the vampire can move up to 10 feet without provoking opportunity attacks.
Legendary Resistance. If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift. As a bonus action, if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and Speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6). As a bonus action, the vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes two Grave Strike attacks and uses Bite.
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) from one of two hands.
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet that is willing or that is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A Humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a Vampire Spawn under the vampire's control.
```

Large elemental, neutral

+3

Entomb. As a bonus action, the scree entombs one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While entombed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the scree, and takes 7 (2d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the scree's turns. The scree can have only one creature entombed at a time. If the scree takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the entombed creature, the scree must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the scree. If the scree dies, an entombed creature breaks free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The scree makes two Avalanche attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Rubble Slide.
Avalanche: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Rubble Slide: One creature within 30 feet must succeed on a DC 14 Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 20 feet straight toward the scree by the rolling wave of stones and be grappled (escape DC 14).

Huge Construct, Lawful Neutral

AC 19

Senses darkvision 120 ft.; Passive Perception 16

Languages Common

CR 17 (XP 18,000; PB +6)

Traits

Brass Rupture. The first time the tyrant is Bloodied in a fight, it releases a burst. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the tyrant. Failure: 35 (10d6) Fire damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Immutable Form. The tyrant can't shape-shift.

Siege Monster. The tyrant deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions

Multiattack. The tyrant makes two Brass Fists attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Molten Brand.

Brass Fists. Melee Attack Roll: +12, reach 10 ft. 20 (4d6 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) Fire damage.

Blast Furnace (Recharge 5-6). Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: 72 (16d8) Fire damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Molten Brand. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, one creature within 60 feet that has the Prone condition. Failure: 71 (13d10) Fire damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Hammer Advance. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Brass Fists attack.

Forge-Wall Breaker. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. During this movement, it can move through Large or smaller objects and structures, destroying them.

Tremor Slam. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature within 10 feet of the tyrant. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

[concept] identity: The swarm hangs in the air above the dying as a churning mass of tarnished funeral bells, and then it falls — a grinding peal of bronze and iron that batters the body while each bell claps itself flat against flesh, seeking the pulse. Every round it re-settles over whoever is closest to death, ringing in rising waves that turn a wound into a death-bell that will not stop, and it does not care which side of the fight the dying are on. When the flock is broken and half its bells lie cracked, it does not scatter — it gathers over its own dead and rings their loss, doubling its tolling into something that can be heard in the marrow.
[concept] lore: They are the funeral bells of a drowned cathedral, torn loose in a flood and never blessed to rest, and they now drift from battlefield to deathbed tolling for anyone they can drag toward the grave.

Medium humanoid, neutral evil

14 (leather)

50 ft.

+2

Skirmish Rider. Krennik ignores difficult terrain, and opportunity attacks against it are made with disadvantage.
Mounted Agility. As a bonus action, Krennik takes the Dash or Disengage action.
Rampage (1/Day). As a bonus action, immediately after dealing damage to a creature that has half of its hit points or fewer, Krennik moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Reaver Lance attack.

Actions
Multiattack: Krennik makes two attacks: one with Reaver Lance and one with Hurled Debris.
Reaver Lance: Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (2d6 + 3) piercing damage. If Krennik moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 4 (1d8) piercing damage and, if it is Medium or smaller, is knocked prone.
Hurled Debris: Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 7 (1d8 + 3) piercing damage.
Trampling Charge (Recharge 5-6): Krennik moves up to its speed without provoking opportunity attacks and can move through the spaces of Medium or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space it enters must succeed on a DC 13 Strength saving throw or take 22 (5d8) bludgeoning damage and be knocked prone.

Multiattack. The lurker makes two Coil attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Opportunist's Lash.

Coil. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 17 (4d6 + 3) Bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Opportunist's Lash. Strength Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 30 feet that is Grappled by the lurker. Failure: 31 (9d6) Piercing damage. Success: Half damage.

Bonus Actions

Smother. The lurker smothers one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While smothered, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the lurker, and takes 7 (2d6) Bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the lurker's turns. The lurker can have only one creature smothered at a time. If the lurker takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the smothered creature, the lurker must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the lurker. If the lurker dies, a smothered creature pulls free Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

It does not toy and it does not flee — once it has a hold it commits, hauling with mindless, patient hunger until the well is quiet again.
[concept] lore: Something fell into this well and never climbed out; it grew in the dark water at the bottom, eating whatever came to drink, and now it lets down no rope but its own coils and waits for the curious.

It will never yield ground; bloodied or not, it returns to the same cinder cone again and again until it or the trespasser is broken. It is mindless (INT 1-2), and it is a territorial guardian that defends its home ground to the death.

Languages Primordial (Auran)

CR 5 (XP 1,800; PB +3)

Traits

Mimicry. The air elemental can mimic animal sounds and humanoid voices. A creature that hears the sounds can tell they are imitations only with a successful DC 13 Wisdom (Insight) check.

Air Form. The air elemental can enter a creature's space and stop there. It can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so.

Actions

Multiattack. The air elemental makes two Thunderous Slam attacks.

Thunderous Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 14 (2d8 + 5) Thunder damage.

Whirlwind (Recharge 4-6). Strength Saving Throw: DC 13, one Medium or smaller creature in the air elemental's space. Failure: 24 (4d10 + 2) Thunder damage, and the target is pushed up to 20 feet straight away from the air elemental and has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Reactive Heads. For each head the hydra has beyond one, it gets an extra reaction that can be used only for opportunity attacks.

[concept] identity: The smoking hydra is a many-headed brute that opens by surging into reach, biting with one head while the others hiss and rear to divide attention. Every round it stays close, trading wounds it never intends to survive on purpose, because each stump it leaves behind vents a choking, stinging haze that punishes anyone pressing the attack. It does not retreat when bloodied; pain only makes it pump out more smoke, and it fights to the last head with a stubborn, hateful patience.
[concept] lore: A hydra that dragged itself out of a smoldering marsh where reeds burn without flame, it hunts to keep its stumps hot and its wounds open, feeding the haze that hides it.

Every round after it drives straight at the thickest obstruction or the clustered enemy, battering with ramming shoulders and whipping buttresses of bound logs, then flinging splintered baulks from its own frame when a target keeps its distance. [concept] lore: A siege-golem is a siege tower that learned to walk: timbers, ram, and mantlet lashed together and animated by a forgotten sapper, still trying to reach and break the wall it was built against.

CR 15 (XP 13,000; PB +5)

Traits

Tunneler. The purple worm can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake.

Actions

Multiattack. The purple worm makes one Bite attack and one Tail Stinger attack.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends.

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 16 (2d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature Grappled by the purple worm (it can have up to three creatures swallowed at a time). Failure: The target is swallowed by the purple worm, and the Grappled condition ends. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the purple worm, and takes 17 (5d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the purple worm's turns.
If the purple worm takes 30 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, the purple worm must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 5 feet of the purple worm and has the Prone condition. If the purple worm dies, any swallowed creature no longer has the Restrained condition and can escape from the corpse using 20 feet of movement, exiting Prone.

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the pit fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the pit fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The pit fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The pit fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The pit fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 18 (3d6 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Fiery Mace. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (4d6 + 8) Force damage plus 21 (6d6) Fire damage.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 26 (4d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The pit fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

Ossuar Archlich
Medium undead, neutral evil
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

315 (42d8 + 126)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


11 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

16 (+3)

	

21 (+5)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Arcana +17, History +11, Insight +8, Perception +8

Saving Throws: 

CON +9, INT +11, WIS +8

Damage Resistances: 

cold, lightning

Damage Immunities: 

necrotic, poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 18

Languages: 

All

Challenge

20 (25,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the archlich fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Spirit Jar. If destroyed, the archlich reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its hit points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within the archlich's lair.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When the archlich is hit by an attack roll, the archlich casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: The archlich makes three attacks, using Eldritch Burst or Paralyzing Touch. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Bestow Curse.
Eldritch Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft., one target. Hit: 31 (4d12 + 5) force damage.
Paralyzing Touch: Melee Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 15 (3d6 + 5) cold damage, and the target is paralyzed until the start of the archlich's next turn.
Spellcasting: The archlich casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 19): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, dispel magic, fireball, invisibility, lightning bolt, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: animate dead, dimension door, plane shift

1/day each: chain lightning, finger of death, power word kill, scrying
Legendary Actions

The archlich can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The archlich regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathly Teleport: The archlich teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and each creature within 10 feet of the space it left takes 11 (2d10) necrotic damage.
Disrupt Life: Each creature that isn't an undead within 20 feet of the archlich must make a DC 19 Constitution saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. Whether it fails or succeeds, the archlich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Frightening Gaze: The archlich casts fear, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. The archlich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

[concept] identity: The archlich does not walk to war; it sits enthroned at the heart of a hollowed sun of bone, an ossuary orb that drifts where the lich wills and from whose lattice it hurls grave-bolts and deathly blasts across the field. Each round it repositions its bone-sun to keep the party in its shadow, casting from behind the ribs of its dead star while the throne drinks the life it scatters. When wounded it grows colder and more patient, spending its hoarded power to raise the sun's ribs as a cage around the nearest foe, and it will not flee — it has nowhere left to die to.
[concept] lore: A god-king who refused the grave, the archlich tore the sun's light out and rebuilt it as a cage of bone; it drifts the dark between worlds, harvesting souls to keep its dead star burning.

Ancient Gold Dragon
Gargantuan dragon, lawful good
Armor Class

22 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

546 (28d20 + 252)

40 ft., fly 80 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

29 (+9)

	

18 (+4)

fire

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 27

Common, Draconic

24 (62,000 XP)

+7

Legendary Resistance (4/Day). If the gold dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Amphibious. The gold dragon can breathe air and water.

Actions
Multiattack: The gold dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Spellcasting to cast Guiding Bolt (level 4 version) or (B) Weakening Breath.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) slashing damage plus 9 (2d8) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The gold dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 24, +16 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: detect magic, guiding bolt, shapechange

1/day each: flame strike, word of recall, zone of truth
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 90-foot cone must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: Each creature that isn't currently affected by this breath in a 90-foot cone must make a DC 24 Strength saving throw. On a failed save, the target has disadvantage on Strength-based d20 tests and subtracts 5 (1d10) from its damage rolls. It repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.
Legendary Actions

The gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Banish: One creature the gold dragon can see within 120 feet of it must succeed on a DC 24 Charisma saving throw or take 24 (7d6) force damage, and the target is incapacitated and is transported to a harmless demiplane until the start of the gold dragon's next turn, at which point it reappears in an unoccupied space of the gold dragon's choice within 120 feet of the gold dragon. Regardless of whether the save succeeds or fails, the gold dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Guiding Light: The gold dragon uses Spellcasting to cast guiding bolt (cast at 4th level).
Pounce: The gold dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

Languages None

CR 9 (XP 5,000; PB +4)

Traits

Caustic Shell. A creature that touches the reaper or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 7 (2d6) Acid damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The reaper makes two attacks, using Spitting Maw or Scythe-Arm in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Broodfear.

Spitting Maw. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 31 (6d8 + 4) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16).

Broodfear. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of the reaper's next turn. If the target was already Frightened by this ability, it also takes 32 (5d12) Acid damage.

Scythe-Arm. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 31 (6d8 + 4) Slashing damage.

Bonus Actions

Cocoon. The reaper cocoons one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While cocooned, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the reaper, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the reaper's turns. The reaper can have only one creature cocooned at a time. If the reaper takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the cocooned creature, the reaper must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the reaper. If the reaper dies, a cocooned creature tears free Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

[concept] identity: A colossal black-iron sentinel plants itself across a chokepoint and refuses to yield, opening with hammering shield bashes and armor-splitting blows from its shield rim and fists. Every round it holds its ground, punishing anyone who tries to slip past with a crushing reactive strike, and it never gives chase — it lets the corridor do the killing. Under pressure it fights to the death without fear or fury, an implacable wall of riveted plate that only stops when its chassis is broken apart.
[concept] lore: Forged in some vanished war-foundry and set to guard a pass, vault, or gate it no longer remembers, the black-iron sentinel stands its post for centuries, treating every living thing that approaches as a trespass to be driven back or broken.

Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When Ilvareth is hit by an attack roll, Ilvareth casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Spellcasting: Ilvareth casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, disguise self, invisibility, light, mage armor, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: fly, lightning bolt

Trip Attack: One creature within 30 feet of Meshrin that is prone must make a DC 15 Dexterity saving throw, taking 28 (8d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.

Riposte (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of Corran misses it with a melee attack, Corran makes one Shortsword attack against that creature.

Actions
Multiattack: Corran makes three Shortsword attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shield Bash.
Shortsword: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d6 + 4) piercing damage, and Corran has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shield Bash: One creature within 5 feet that Corran can see must make a DC 15 Strength saving throw, taking 9 (2d4 + 4) bludgeoning damage on a failed save. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it is knocked prone.

[concept] identity: A sellsword who opens every fight by hurling a handaxe to stagger the nearest foe, then closes the gap with a shortsword before the thrown weapon has finished ringing off shield or skull. A mercenary to the bone, it fights for coin rather than death, breaking off and withdrawing when the odds turn bloody.
[concept] lore: A cut-price blade sold from one border war to the next, carrying a wood-hafted axe for opening ground and a shortsword for the bill afterward.
